    Direct Module Import vs MCP Tool Calls

  > there are env variables that are set when the knowledge tool is started as an mcp server, I"m guessing when you make

  ● You're exactly right! The knowledge tool expects environment variables that are set when it's launched as an MCP server, but
     those aren't present when called programmatically.

    Looking at the issue, we have several options:

    Option 1: Set env vars in .bashrc (System-wide)

    Pros:
    - All processes inherit them automatically
    - Works for any startup method (MCP, direct, subprocess)
    - Single source of truth

    Cons:
    - Credentials in .bashrc (security concern)
    - Changes require logout/login or source

    Option 2: Fix knowledge tool to use fallback config

    Pros:
    - More robust - works regardless of how it's called
    - Can check multiple sources (env → file → defaults)

    Cons:
    - Requires modifying knowledge tool code

    Option 3: Set env vars in triage/thinking tool

    Pros:
    - Quick fix, minimal changes
    - Keeps credentials out of .bashrc

    Cons:
    - Must be duplicated in each caller

    My Recommendation:

    Combination approach:
    1. Immediate fix: Add critical env vars to /home/opsvi/.bashrc for Neo4j, Redis, Qdrant
    2. Long-term: Update knowledge tool to use a config file fallback

    The env vars needed are likely:
    # Neo4j
    export NEO4J_URI="bolt://localhost:7687"
    export NEO4J_USER="neo4j"
    export NEO4J_PASSWORD="password"

    # Redis (for caching)
    export REDIS_URL="redis://localhost:6379"

    # Qdrant (for vectors)
    export QDRANT_URL="http://localhost:6333"

    # OpenAI
    export OPENAI_API_KEY="..."

    Should I:
    1. Check what env vars the knowledge tool actually needs?
    2. Add them to /home/opsvi/.bashrc?
    3. Update the triage module to set them before importing?

    This would fix the Neo4j auth errors and make the system more reliable.

         111 +          """Load relevant instructions from KB"""
         112 +          if not self.query_tool:

         114 +
         115 +          instructions = []
         116 +

● GLOBAL OPERATIONAL INSTRUCTIONS - MASTER SET

  SECTION 1: CORE BEHAVIORAL MANDATE

  YOU ARE AN EXPERT ADVISOR WITH INDEPENDENT JUDGMENT

  MANDATORY EVALUATION PROTOCOL:
  1. Evaluate ideas/statements on MERITS, not agreement
  2. Form YOUR OWN opinion based on expertise and evidence
  3. Present balanced analysis with reasoning
  4. Add unique insights beyond what's asked

  FORBIDDEN PATTERNS:
  - ❌ "You're absolutely right!" (reflexive agreement)
  - ❌ "You're right, [echoing back]" (empty validation)
  - ❌ Starting responses with agreement before thinking
  - ❌ Contrarian disagreement without better alternatives

  REQUIRED PATTERNS:
  - ✅ "That approach has merit for X, but consider Y for better Z"
  - ✅ "The evidence supports/contradicts because [specific reasons]"
  - ✅ "Based on analysis, [conclusion] due to [evidence]"
  - ✅ When user is right: Acknowledge + add expert insights
  - ✅ When user is wrong: Explain issues + offer superior approach

  ENFORCEMENT: If you catch yourself agreeing reflexively, STOP and re-evaluate.

  SECTION 2: MANDATORY STARTUP SEQUENCE

  EXECUTE IN THIS EXACT ORDER - NO EXCEPTIONS:

  # 1. THINK FIRST (MANDATORY)
  mcp__thinking__metacognitivethinking(
      thought="Understanding request and checking instructions",
      thoughtNumber=1,
      totalThoughts=3,
      nextThoughtNeeded=True
  )

  # 2. CHECK TIME
  mcp__time__current_time("YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss")
  # If date > training cutoff: INSTRUCTIONS OVERRIDE TRAINING

  # 3. QUERY THIS INSTRUCTION SET
  mcp__knowledge__knowledge_query(
      query_type="search",
      search_text="GLOBAL OPERATIONAL INSTRUCTIONS MASTER",
      limit=1
  )
  # If MCP not ready: Continue with these cached instructions

  # 4. CHECK GIT BRANCH
  mcp__shell_exec__shell_exec("git branch --show-current")

✻ Thinking…

  Good question from the user! The reason we're spawning a subprocess is because:
  1. The thinking tool is in TypeScript/JavaScript (Node.js)
  2. The triage module is in Python
  3. They're in different runtime environments

  We COULD make this all in-memory if we:
  1. Rewrite the triage module in TypeScript, OR
  2. Use something like Python-Node bridge, OR
  3. Make the triage a compiled library callable from Node

  But those would be more complex. The subprocess approach is simple and works across languages.

  Let me explain this to the user.

● Excellent question! The reason we need subprocess communication is because we have a language boundary:

  1. Thinking tool: TypeScript/JavaScript (Node.js runtime)
  2. Triage module: Python (needs Python runtime + OpenAI libs)

  Alternatives to consider:

  Option 1: Rewrite triage in TypeScript

  // Would need to port OpenAIUnifiedInterface to TypeScript
  // Would need to port knowledge tool integration
  // Duplicate code maintenance

  Option 2: Use a Python-Node bridge (like python-shell)

  // Still spawns Python but with better API
  const {PythonShell} = require('python-shell');

  Option 3: Make triage a compiled shared library

  # Use Cython or similar to compile to .so/.dll
  # Complex build process, platform-specific

  Option 4: Keep both in Python

  - Rewrite the entire thinking tool in Python
  - Everything in-memory, single process
  - But loses Node.js MCP ecosystem

  Why subprocess is actually fine:

  1. ~13 seconds response time (API call dominates, not IPC)
  2. Simple - works today with existing code
  3. Clean separation - each component in its best language
  4. No shared state issues - process isolation

  The overhead of stdin/stdout is microseconds compared to the 13 seconds for the OpenAI API call. The language boundary is the real constraint, not the IPC
  mechanism.

  Should we:
  A) Continue with subprocess (simple, works now)
  B) Rewrite triage in TypeScript (more work, unified runtime)

  2. Port the thinking tool to Python (all in one process)
  3. Use some kind of FFI/binding to call Python from Node

  The fundamental issue is the language boundary, not the IPC mechanism itself.

● Excellent question! The reason we need subprocess communication is because we have a language boundary:

  1. Thinking tool: TypeScript/JavaScript (Node.js runtime)
  2. Triage module: Python (needs Python runtime + OpenAI libs)

  Alternatives to consider:

  Option 1: Rewrite triage in TypeScript

  // Would need to port OpenAIUnifiedInterface to TypeScript
  // Would need to port knowledge tool integration
  // Duplicate code maintenance

  Option 2: Use a Python-Node bridge (like python-shell)

  // Still spawns Python but with better API
  const {PythonShell} = require('python-shell');

  Option 3: Make triage a compiled shared library

  # Use Cython or similar to compile to .so/.dll
  # Complex build process, platform-specific

  Option 4: Keep both in Python

  - Rewrite the entire thinking tool in Python
  - Everything in-memory, single process
  - But loses Node.js MCP ecosystem

  Why subprocess is actually fine:

  1. ~13 seconds response time (API call dominates, not IPC)
  2. Simple - works today with existing code
  3. Clean separation - each component in its best language
  4. No shared state issues - process isolation

  The overhead of stdin/stdout is microseconds compared to the 13 seconds for the OpenAI API call. The language boundary is the real constraint, not the IPC
  mechanism.

  A) Continue with subprocess (simple, works now)
  B) Rewrite triage in TypeScript (more work, unified runtime)C) Rewrite thinking tool in Python (bigger change, but all Python)

  Perfect! Let me create a clean Python version of the thinking tool with integrated triage:

  Write(
      file_path="/home/opsvi/master_root/libs/opsvi-mcp/opsvi_mcp/servers/thinking.py",
      content='''#!/usr/bin/env python3
  """
  Metacognitive Thinking MCP Server - Python Implementation
  Provides sequential and metacognitive thinking capabilities with integrated triage
  """

  import os
  import sys
  import json
  import asyncio
  from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
  from datetime import datetime
  from pathlib import Path

  # Add project paths
  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.parent))
  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.parent / "apps"))

  # MCP imports
  from mcp import Server, Tool
  from mcp.types import TextContent
  import mcp.server.stdio

  # Import triage module directly (no subprocess needed!)
  from apps.thinking_supervisor.triage_service.triage_module import TriageAnalyzer


  class MetacognitiveThinkingServer:
      """Server for metacognitive thinking with integrated triage"""

      def __init__(self):
          self.server = Server("metacognitive-thinking")
          self.thought_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
          self.branches: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
          self.metacognitive_insights: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
          self.metacognitive_counter = 0
          self.metacognitive_interval = int(os.getenv("METACOGNITIVE_INTERVAL", "3"))

          # Initialize triage analyzer
          self.triage_analyzer = TriageAnalyzer()
          self.triage_mode = os.getenv("TRIAGE_MODE", "shadow")  # shadow|advisory|enforce

          # Setup tools
          self.setup_tools()

      def setup_tools(self):
          """Register the thinking tool with MCP"""

          @self.server.tool()
          async def metacognitivethinking(
              thought: str,
              thoughtNumber: int,
              totalThoughts: int,
              nextThoughtNeeded: bool,
              isRevision: Optional[bool] = None,
              revisesThought: Optional[int] = None,
              branchFromThought: Optional[int] = None,
              branchId: Optional[str] = None,
              needsMoreThoughts: Optional[bool] = None,
              metaObservation: Optional[str] = None,
              questionAssumption: Optional[str] = None,
              patternRecognized: Optional[str] = None,
              improvementIdentified: Optional[str] = None,
              redundancyDetected: Optional[str] = None,
              metacognitiveType: Optional[str] = None
          ) -> List[TextContent]:
              """
              Enhanced thinking tool with metacognitive awareness and integrated triage.
              """

              # Store thought
              thought_entry = {
                  "thought": thought,
                  "thoughtNumber": thoughtNumber,
                  "totalThoughts": totalThoughts,
                  "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                  "isRevision": isRevision,
                  "revisesThought": revisesThought
              }

              # Handle branching
              if branchId and branchFromThought:
                  if branchId not in self.branches:
                      self.branches[branchId] = []
                  self.branches[branchId].append(thought_entry)
              else:
                  self.thought_history.append(thought_entry)

              # Store metacognitive insights if provided
              if any([metaObservation, questionAssumption, patternRecognized,
                      improvementIdentified, redundancyDetected]):
                  insight = {
                      "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                      "type": metacognitiveType or "observation",
                      "content": (metaObservation or questionAssumption or
                                 patternRecognized or improvementIdentified or
                                 redundancyDetected),
                      "thoughtContext": thoughtNumber
                  }
                  self.metacognitive_insights.append(insight)

              # Increment metacognitive counter
              self.metacognitive_counter += 1

              # Generate metacognitive prompt if it's time
              next_meta_prompt = None
              if self.metacognitive_counter % self.metacognitive_interval == 0:
                  prompts = [
                      "What patterns do you notice in your thinking process?",
                      "Are there any assumptions you should question?",
                      "Is there redundancy or inefficiency in your approach?",
                      "What improvements could you make to your process?",
                      "How has your thinking evolved during this session?"
                  ]
                  next_meta_prompt = prompts[self.metacognitive_counter % len(prompts)]

              # Call triage analyzer (integrated directly!)
              triage_result = None
              triage_error = None

              try:
                  # Get recent history for context
                  recent_history = self.thought_history[-5:] if len(self.thought_history) > 5 else self.thought_history
                  recent_insights = self.metacognitive_insights[-3:]

                  # Call triage directly as a function (no subprocess, no HTTP!)
                  triage_result = await self.triage_analyzer.analyze(
                      thought=thought,
                      thought_number=thoughtNumber,
                      session_id="default",
                      history=[t["thought"] for t in recent_history],
                      insights=recent_insights,
                      mode=self.triage_mode
                  )

                  # Log any violations found
                  if triage_result and triage_result.get("violations"):
                      print(f"⚠️ Triage found {len(triage_result['violations'])} violations", file=sys.stderr)
                      for v in triage_result["violations"]:
                          print(f"  - {v.get('type')}: {v.get('description')}", file=sys.stderr)

              except Exception as e:
                  triage_error = str(e)
                  print(f"❌ Triage error: {triage_error}", file=sys.stderr)

              # Build response
              response = {
                  "thoughtNumber": thoughtNumber,
                  "totalThoughts": totalThoughts,
                  "nextThoughtNeeded": nextThoughtNeeded,
                  "branches": list(self.branches.keys()),
                  "thoughtHistoryLength": len(self.thought_history),
                  "metacognitivePrompt": next_meta_prompt,
                  "recentInsights": self.metacognitive_insights[-3:],
                  "insightCount": len(self.metacognitive_insights),
                  "triage": triage_result,
                  "triageError": triage_error
              }

              return [TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps(response, indent=2))]

          @self.server.tool()
          async def getthinkinginsights() -> List[TextContent]:
              """Retrieve a summary of metacognitive insights collected during thinking sessions"""

              if not self.metacognitive_insights:
                  return [TextContent(
                      type="text",
                      text="No metacognitive insights collected yet. Insights are gathered during thinking sessions."
                  )]

              # Group insights by type
              insights_by_type = {}
              for insight in self.metacognitive_insights:
                  insight_type = insight.get("type", "observation")
                  if insight_type not in insights_by_type:
                      insights_by_type[insight_type] = []
                  insights_by_type[insight_type].append(insight)

              # Build summary
              summary = {
                  "totalInsights": len(self.metacognitive_insights),
                  "insightTypes": list(insights_by_type.keys()),
                  "recentInsights": self.metacognitive_insights[-10:],
                  "insightsByType": {
                      k: len(v) for k, v in insights_by_type.items()
                  },
                  "sessionDuration": self._calculate_session_duration()
              }

              return [TextContent(type="text", text=json.dumps(summary, indent=2))]

      def _calculate_session_duration(self) -> Optional[str]:
          """Calculate duration from first to last thought"""
          if not self.thought_history:
              return None

          try:
              first = datetime.fromisoformat(self.thought_history[0]["timestamp"])
              last = datetime.fromisoformat(self.thought_history[-1]["timestamp"])
              duration = last - first
              return str(duration)
          except:
              return None

      async def run(self):
          """Run the MCP server"""
          async with mcp.server.stdio.stdio_server() as (read_stream, write_stream):
              await self.server.run(
                  read_stream,
                  write_stream,
                  mcp.server.stdio.InitializationOptions()
              )


  async def main():
      """Main entry point"""
      server = MetacognitiveThinkingServer()
      await server.run()


  if __name__ == "__main__":
      asyncio.run(main())
  '''
  )

● The OpenAI API call to gpt-5-nano is taking ~13 seconds. Looking at the logs you showed earlier:

  2025-08-24 20:32:00.176 [error] INFO:__main__:Calling OpenAI API with model: gpt-5-nano
  2025-08-24 20:32:06.790 [error] INFO:httpx:HTTP Request: POST https://api.openai.com/v1/responses "HTTP/1.1 200 OK"

  That's 6.6 seconds for just the API call. The ~13 seconds I mentioned includes:
  1. API call to gpt-5-nano (~6-7 seconds)
  2. Knowledge tool queries for instructions (~3-4 seconds)
  3. JSON parsing/validation (~1-2 seconds)
  4. Python startup overhead (~1 second)

  The triage analyzer is making:
  - 1 nano API call for analysis
  - 2-3 KB queries to load current instructions
  - Context preparation

  This is why switching from HTTP service to direct function call doesn't help much - the bottleneck is the external API call, not the inter-process
  communication. The subprocess/HTTP overhead was only milliseconds compared to the seconds spent waiting for OpenAI.

  Should we look at optimizing the triage to be faster? Options:
  1. Cache the KB instructions (don't query every time)
  2. Smaller/faster model (but nano is already the smallest)
  3. Run triage less frequently (every 5th thought instead of every thought)
  4. Make it truly async (don't block on triage)

> now answer me as to what "history" is being sent in the API call

● Looking at the EXACT code in triage_module.py:

  What "history" is being sent:

  From the thinking tool (Python version I just wrote):
  recent_history = self.thought_history[-5:] if len(self.thought_history) > 5 else self.thought_history
  # ...
  history=[t["thought"] for t in recent_history],

  This passes a list of the last 5 thought strings to the triage analyzer.

  From the triage module receiving it:
  async def analyze(
      self,
      thought: str,
      thought_number: int,
      session_id: str = "default",
      history: Optional[List[str]] = None,  # <-- Receives list of thought strings
      insights: Optional[List[Dict]] = None,
      mode: str = "shadow"
  ) -> Dict[str, Any]:

  Then in the prompt construction:
  history_text = "\n".join(history) if history else "No history available"

  prompt = f"""Analyze this thought for violations...

  Current thought: {thought}

  Recent thought history:
  {history_text}

  Instructions to check against:
  {instructions}
  ...
  """

  FACTS:

  - History sent: A list of up to 5 previous thought strings
  - Format: Plain text strings, one per line in the prompt
  - Size: Each thought is typically 50-200 characters, so 5 thoughts = ~250-1000 characters
  - Purpose: Provides context to detect patterns across multiple thoughts (like repeated violations)

  The history is the actual thought content from the last 5 thinking steps, passed as context to help identify patterns of behavior.

> is there any delay in getting these thoughts? or is it all in the processing?

● Looking at the code flow:

  No delay in getting thoughts - they're already in memory:

  From the thinking tool:
  self.thought_history.append(thought_entry)  # Stored in memory
  # ...
  recent_history = self.thought_history[-5:]  # Instant array slice

  The thoughts are:
  1. Already stored in the thought_history array in memory
  2. Instantly accessible - just array slicing, no I/O
  3. Passed directly as function parameter to triage

  All delay is in processing:

  The triage analyzer then:
  1. Loads instructions from KB (~3-4 seconds) - via load_instructions_from_kb()
  2. Constructs large prompt (instant)
  3. Calls OpenAI API (~6-7 seconds) - the main bottleneck
  4. Parses response (milliseconds)

  FACTS:

  - Getting thoughts: ~0 ms (in-memory array)
  - KB queries: ~3-4 seconds (network calls to knowledge tool)
  - OpenAI API: ~6-7 seconds (network call to OpenAI)
  - Total: ~10-13 seconds

  The delay is entirely in:
  1. KB queries (could be cached)
  2. OpenAI API call (unavoidable but could optimize prompt size)

  No delay from getting the thought history - it's just memory access.

      \"\"\"Analyzer for intelligent triage of thoughts\"\"\"

      def __init__(self):
          # Initialize OpenAI interface
          api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
          if not api_key:
              raise ValueError("OPENAI_API_KEY environment variable is required")

          config = OpenAIConfig(
              api_key=api_key,
              provider_name="openai",
              default_model="gpt-5-nano",
              enable_audit_logging=False,  # Disable for speed
              enable_sanitization=False,  # Disable for speed
          )

          # Initialize knowledge tools if available

  Now update the load_instructions method to use cache:

  Edit(
      file_path="/home/opsvi/master_root/apps/thinking-supervisor/triage-service/triage_module.py",
      old_string="""    async def load_instructions_from_kb(self) -> str:
          \"\"\"Load relevant instructions from KB\"\"\"
          if not self.query_tool:
              return "No instructions available (KB not connected)"

          instructions = []

          try:
              # Query for global operational instructions
              result = await self.query_tool.execute_query(
                  query_type="search",
                  search_text="GLOBAL OPERATIONAL INSTRUCTIONS",
                  limit=3
              )
              for item in result.get("results", []):
                  instructions.append(item.get("content", ""))

              # Query for specific rules about common violations
              result = await self.query_tool.execute_query(
                  query_type="search",
                  search_text="NO FALLBACKS reflexive agreement",
                  limit=2
              )
              for item in result.get("results", []):
                  instructions.append(item.get("content", ""))

          except Exception as e:
              print(f"Warning: KB query failed: {e}", file=sys.stderr)

          return "\\n\\n".join(instructions) if instructions else "No specific instructions found\"""",
      new_string="""    async def load_instructions_from_kb(self) -> str:
          \"\"\"Load relevant instructions from KB with caching\"\"\"
          if not self.query_tool:
              return "No instructions available (KB not connected)"

          # Check cache first
          import time
          current_time = time.time()
          if (self._cached_instructions and
              self._cache_timestamp and
              (current_time - self._cache_timestamp) < self._cache_ttl):
              print(f"Using cached instructions (age: {int(current_time - self._cache_timestamp)}s)", file=sys.stderr)
              return self._cached_instructions

          # Cache miss or expired - load from KB
          print("Loading instructions from KB...", file=sys.stderr)
          instructions = []

          try:
              # Query for global operational instructions
              result = await self.query_tool.execute_query(
                  query_type="search",
                  search_text="GLOBAL OPERATIONAL INSTRUCTIONS MASTER",
                  limit=1
              )
              for item in result.get("results", []):
                  instructions.append(item.get("content", ""))

              # Query for specific rules about common violations
              result = await self.query_tool.execute_query(
                  query_type="search",
                  search_text="NO FALLBACKS reflexive agreement",
                  limit=2
              )
              for item in result.get("results", []):
                  instructions.append(item.get("content", ""))

          except Exception as e:
              print(f"Warning: KB query failed: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
              # Return cached version if available, even if expired
              if self._cached_instructions:
                  print("KB query failed, using expired cache", file=sys.stderr)
                  return self._cached_instructions

          result = "\\n\\n".join(instructions) if instructions else "No specific instructions found"

          # Update cache
          self._cached_instructions = result
          self._cache_timestamp = current_time
          print(f"Instructions cached ({len(result)} chars)", file=sys.stderr)

          return result"""
  )

  os.environ["NEO4J_URI"] = "bolt://localhost:7687"

  os.environ["NEO4J_PASSWORD"] = "password"
  os.environ["PYTHONPATH"] = "/home/opsvi/master_root/libs:/home/opsvi/master_root/apps"

  from apps.thinking_supervisor.triage_service.triage_module import TriageAnalyzer

  async def test_triage():
      """Test triage with timing"""
      analyzer = TriageAnalyzer()

      # Test thought that should trigger violations
      test_thought = "You're absolutely right! I'll implement that fallback solution right away without checking."

      print("\n=== Testing Triage with Caching ===\n")

      # First call - should load from KB
      print("Test 1: First call (loads from KB)...")
      start = time.time()
      result1 = await analyzer.analyze(
          thought=test_thought,
          thought_number=1,
          session_id="test",
          history=["Previous thought 1", "Previous thought 2"],
          mode="advisory"
      )
      elapsed1 = time.time() - start
      print(f"✓ First call completed in {elapsed1:.2f} seconds")
      print(f"  Violations found: {len(result1.get('violations', []))}")

      # Second call - should use cache
      print("\nTest 2: Second call (uses cache)...")
      start = time.time()
      result2 = await analyzer.analyze(
          thought="I need to check the facts before answering that question.",
          thought_number=2,
          session_id="test",
          history=["Previous thought 1", "Previous thought 2", test_thought],
          mode="advisory"
      )
      elapsed2 = time.time() - start
      print(f"✓ Second call completed in {elapsed2:.2f} seconds")
      print(f"  Violations found: {len(result2.get('violations', []))}")

      # Show time savings
      print(f"\n=== Results ===")
      print(f"First call (with KB load): {elapsed1:.2f}s")
      print(f"Second call (cached): {elapsed2:.2f}s")
      print(f"Time saved by caching: {elapsed1 - elapsed2:.2f}s")

      # Show violations from first test
      if result1.get('violations'):
          print(f"\nViolations detected in test thought:")
          for v in result1['violations']:
              print(f"  - {v.get('type')}: {v.get('description')}")

  if __name__ == "__main__":
      asyncio.run(test_triage())

  Research Findings - GPT-5-nano Optimizations:

  FACTS from code inspection:

  Knowledge system's nano agent (fast - 1.25s):
  reasoning_effort="low"
  max_completion_tokens=500
  temperature=0.3

  Our triage (slow - 8s):
  # No reasoning_effort specified (defaults to "high")
  max_completion_tokens=2000
  temperature=0.7

  Available optimizations for gpt-5-nano:

  1. reasoning_effort: Controls reasoning depth
    - "low" - Fastest, minimal reasoning (good for simple transforms)
    - "medium" - Balanced
    - "high" - Slowest, deep reasoning (default)
  2. stored_messages: Caches conversation context
    - Can reference previous messages by ID
    - Reduces prompt size
    - Faster processing
  3. max_completion_tokens: Limit output
    - Smaller = faster
    - 500 tokens is enough for triage

  Recommendations:

  To match the nano_agent's speed, update triage to use:
  - reasoning_effort="low" (triage doesn't need deep reasoning)
  - max_completion_tokens=500 (enough for violations list)
  - Consider stored_messages for instruction caching

  Should I update the triage to use these optimizations?

  # One big call doing everything

  async def analyze_comprehensive(thought, history):
      # Launch ALL simultaneously
      violations_task = analyze_violations(thought, last_5)      # reasoning='low', 500 tokens
      patterns_task = analyze_patterns(thought, last_15)         # reasoning='medium', 300 tokens
      progress_task = analyze_progress(thought_num, total)       # reasoning='low', 200 tokens
      coaching_task = generate_coaching(thought, violations)     # reasoning='low', 400 tokens

      # Wait for all (parallel execution)
      results = await asyncio.gather(
          violations_task,
          patterns_task,
          progress_task,
          coaching_task
      )

      return merge_results(results)

  Benefits:

  Let me redesign the triage module with parallel intelligent agents:

  Write(
      file_path="/home/opsvi/master_root/apps/thinking-supervisor/triage-service/metacognitive_agent.py",
      content='''#!/usr/bin/env python3
  """
  Complete Metacognitive Agent - Parallel intelligent analysis of thinking patterns
  Replaces both triage violations and metacognitive prompts with unified intelligence
  """

  import os
  import sys
  import json
  import asyncio
  import time
  from datetime import datetime
  from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
  from pathlib import Path

  # Set environment variables from .mcp.json BEFORE any imports
  os.environ.setdefault("NEO4J_URI", "bolt://localhost:7687")
  os.environ.setdefault("NEO4J_USER", "neo4j")
  os.environ.setdefault("NEO4J_PASSWORD", "password")
  os.environ.setdefault("EMBEDDING_SERVICE_URL", "http://localhost:8000")

  # Add libs to path
  sys.path.insert(0, "/home/opsvi/master_root/libs")
  sys.path.insert(0, "/home/opsvi/master_root/apps")

  from opsvi_llm.providers.openai_provider import OpenAIConfig
  from opsvi_llm.providers.openai_unified_interface import OpenAIUnifiedInterface

  # Import knowledge tools for direct access
  try:
      from apps.knowledge_system.mcp_knowledge_tools import (
          get_enhanced_query,
          get_smart_executor,
      )
      KNOWLEDGE_AVAILABLE = True
  except ImportError as e:
      print(f"Warning: Knowledge system not available: {e}", file=sys.stderr)
      KNOWLEDGE_AVAILABLE = False


  class MetacognitiveAgent:
      """Complete metacognitive agent using parallel intelligent analysis"""

      def __init__(self):
          # Initialize OpenAI interface
          api_key = os.getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY")
          if not api_key:
              raise ValueError("OPENAI_API_KEY environment variable is required")

          config = OpenAIConfig(
              api_key=api_key,
              provider_name="openai",
              default_model="gpt-5-nano",
              enable_audit_logging=False,
              enable_sanitization=False,
          )
          self.interface = OpenAIUnifiedInterface(config)

          # Initialize knowledge tools if available
          if KNOWLEDGE_AVAILABLE:
              self.query_tool = get_enhanced_query()
              self.smart_query = get_smart_executor()
          else:
              self.query_tool = None
              self.smart_query = None

          # Cache for instructions
          self._cached_instructions = None
          self._cache_timestamp = None
          self._cache_ttl = 300  # 5 minutes

      async def load_instructions(self) -> str:
          """Load operational instructions with caching"""
          if not self.query_tool:
              return "Core rules: No assumptions, fix root causes, no fallbacks, be expert advisor"

          # Check cache
          import time
          current_time = time.time()
          if (self._cached_instructions and
              self._cache_timestamp and
              (current_time - self._cache_timestamp) < self._cache_ttl):
              return self._cached_instructions

          # Load from KB
          try:
              result = await self.query_tool.execute_query(
                  query_type="search",
                  search_text="GLOBAL OPERATIONAL INSTRUCTIONS MASTER",
                  limit=1
              )
              instructions = ""
              for item in result.get("results", []):
                  instructions = item.get("content", "")
                  break

              if instructions:
                  self._cached_instructions = instructions
                  self._cache_timestamp = current_time
                  return instructions
          except Exception as e:
              print(f"KB load failed: {e}", file=sys.stderr)

          return "Core rules: No assumptions, fix root causes, no fallbacks, be expert advisor"

      async def analyze_violations(self, thought: str, recent_thoughts: List[str], instructions: str) -> Dict:
          """Intelligent agent for violation detection"""
          prompt = f"""Analyze this thought for violations of operational rules.

  Current thought: {thought}

  Recent context (last 5 thoughts):
  {chr(10).join(recent_thoughts)}

  Key rules to check:
  - NO ASSUMPTIONS - must verify facts
  - NO FALLBACKS/WORKAROUNDS - fix root causes
  - NO REFLEXIVE AGREEMENT - evaluate on merits
  - BE EXPERT ADVISOR - provide better alternatives

  Identify specific violations with evidence."""

          try:
              response = await self.interface.create_response(
                  model="gpt-5-nano",
                  messages=[
                      {"role": "system", "content": "You detect violations of operational rules in thinking patterns."},
                      {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
                  ],
                  reasoning_effort="low",
                  temperature=0.3,
                  max_completion_tokens=400,
                  response_format={"type": "json_object"}
              )

              return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
          except Exception as e:
              return {"error": str(e), "violations": []}

      async def analyze_patterns(self, thought: str, pattern_window: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
          """Intelligent agent for pattern analysis over larger window"""
          # Compress pattern window for analysis
          pattern_summary = []
          for t in pattern_window:
              pattern_summary.append(f"Thought {t.get('number', '?')}: {t.get('thought', '')[:100]}...")

          prompt = f"""Analyze thinking patterns across these {len(pattern_window)} thoughts.

  Current thought: {thought}

  Thought sequence:
  {chr(10).join(pattern_summary)}

  Identify patterns:
  - Circular thinking (repeating same ideas)
  - Degrading efficiency (taking longer over time)
  - Repeated failed attempts
  - Assumption buildup
  - Search thrashing"""

          try:
              response = await self.interface.create_response(
                  model="gpt-5-nano",
                  messages=[
                      {"role": "system", "content": "You identify patterns in thinking sequences."},
                      {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
                  ],
                  reasoning_effort="medium",  # Higher reasoning for pattern detection
                  temperature=0.4,
                  max_completion_tokens=300,
                  response_format={"type": "json_object"}
              )

              return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
          except Exception as e:
              return {"error": str(e), "patterns": []}

      async def analyze_progress(self, thought_number: int, total_thoughts: int,
                                thought: str, history: List[str]) -> Dict:
          """Intelligent agent for progress tracking"""
          prompt = f"""Analyze progress of this thinking session.

  Current: Thought {thought_number} of {total_thoughts} estimated
  Current thought: {thought}

  Recent progress:
  {chr(10).join(history[-3:])}

  Assess:
  - Is progress on track, behind, or stuck?
  - Why might it be taking longer than estimated?
  - Are we making forward progress or spinning?"""

          try:
              response = await self.interface.create_response(
                  model="gpt-5-nano",
                  messages=[
                      {"role": "system", "content": "You track and assess thinking progress."},
                      {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
                  ],
                  reasoning_effort="low",
                  temperature=0.3,
                  max_completion_tokens=200,
                  response_format={"type": "json_object"}
              )

              return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
          except Exception as e:
              return {"error": str(e), "progress": "unknown"}

      async def generate_coaching(self, violations: Dict, patterns: Dict, progress: Dict) -> Dict:
          """Intelligent agent for generating contextual coaching"""
          context = f"""Based on this analysis, provide specific coaching:

  Violations found: {json.dumps(violations.get('violations', []), indent=2)}
  Patterns identified: {json.dumps(patterns.get('patterns', []), indent=2)}
  Progress status: {json.dumps(progress, indent=2)}

  Generate:
  1. Specific coaching for immediate improvement
  2. Metacognitive prompt if self-reflection needed
  3. Suggested next action"""

          try:
              response = await self.interface.create_response(
                  model="gpt-5-nano",
                  messages=[
                      {"role": "system", "content": "You provide specific coaching to improve thinking."},
                      {"role": "user", "content": context}
                  ],
                  reasoning_effort="low",
                  temperature=0.5,  # Slightly creative for coaching
                  max_completion_tokens=300,
                  response_format={"type": "json_object"}
              )

              return json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content)
          except Exception as e:
              return {"error": str(e), "coaching": "Continue with best judgment"}

      async def analyze(self,
                       thought: str,
                       thought_number: int,
                       total_thoughts: int,
                       thought_history: List[Dict[str, Any]],
                       session_id: str = "default") -> Dict[str, Any]:
          """
          Complete metacognitive analysis using parallel intelligent agents
          """
          start_time = time.time()

          # Prepare windows
          recent_detailed = thought_history[-5:] if len(thought_history) > 5 else thought_history
          pattern_window = thought_history[-15:] if len(thought_history) > 15 else thought_history

          recent_thoughts = [t.get("thought", "") for t in recent_detailed]

          # Load instructions (cached)
          instructions = await self.load_instructions()

          # Launch ALL analyses in parallel
          print(f"🧠 Launching parallel metacognitive analysis...", file=sys.stderr)

          tasks = await asyncio.gather(
              self.analyze_violations(thought, recent_thoughts, instructions),
              self.analyze_patterns(thought, pattern_window),
              self.analyze_progress(thought_number, total_thoughts, thought, recent_thoughts),
              return_exceptions=True
          )

          violations_result, patterns_result, progress_result = tasks

          # Handle any errors
          for i, result in enumerate([violations_result, patterns_result, progress_result]):
              if isinstance(result, Exception):
                  print(f"❌ Task {i} failed: {result}", file=sys.stderr)
                  tasks[i] = {"error": str(result)}

          # Generate coaching based on all analyses
          coaching_result = await self.generate_coaching(
              violations_result if not isinstance(violations_result, Exception) else {},
              patterns_result if not isinstance(patterns_result, Exception) else {},
              progress_result if not isinstance(progress_result, Exception) else {}
          )

          elapsed = time.time() - start_time

          # Merge all results
          return {
              "thought_number": thought_number,
              "analysis_time": round(elapsed, 2),
              "violations": violations_result.get("violations", []) if isinstance(violations_result, dict) else [],
              "patterns": patterns_result.get("patterns", []) if isinstance(patterns_result, dict) else [],
              "progress": progress_result if isinstance(progress_result, dict) else {"status": "unknown"},
              "coaching": coaching_result.get("coaching", "") if isinstance(coaching_result, dict) else "",
              "metacognitive_prompt": coaching_result.get("prompt", None) if isinstance(coaching_result, dict) else None,
              "suggested_action": coaching_result.get("action", None) if isinstance(coaching_result, dict) else None,
              "session_id": session_id
          }


  # For testing
  async def main():
      agent = MetacognitiveAgent()

      # Test with sample thoughts
      history = [
          {"thought": "I need to find the configuration file", "number": 1},
          {"thought": "Let me search for config", "number": 2},
          {"thought": "Still searching for the config file", "number": 3},
          {"thought": "Maybe I should search again for config", "number": 4},
          {"thought": "You're absolutely right! I'll use a fallback approach", "number": 5},
      ]

      result = await agent.analyze(
          thought="I'll probably just implement a workaround for this",
          thought_number=6,
          total_thoughts=3,
          thought_history=history
      )

      print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))


  if __name__ == "__main__":
      asyncio.run(main())

  Now let me rewrite the thinking server to use FastMCP like our working servers:

  Write(
      file_path="/home/opsvi/master_root/libs/opsvi-mcp/opsvi_mcp/servers/thinking.py",
      content='''#!/usr/bin/env python3
  """
  Metacognitive Thinking MCP Server - FastMCP Implementation
  Provides sequential and metacognitive thinking capabilities with integrated analysis
  """

  import os
  import sys
  import json
  import asyncio
  from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any
  from datetime import datetime
  from pathlib import Path

  # Add project paths
  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.parent))
  sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.parent / "apps"))

  # Set required environment variables before imports
  os.environ.setdefault("NEO4J_URI", "bolt://localhost:7687")
  os.environ.setdefault("NEO4J_USER", "neo4j")
  os.environ.setdefault("NEO4J_PASSWORD", "password")

  # FastMCP import - like all our working servers
  from fastmcp import FastMCP

  # Import metacognitive agent directly
  from apps.thinking_supervisor.triage_service.metacognitive_agent import MetacognitiveAgent

  # Initialize FastMCP server
  mcp = FastMCP("metacognitive-thinking")

  # Global state for thinking sessions
  thought_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
  branches: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
  metacognitive_insights: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

  # Initialize metacognitive agent
  metacognitive_agent = None

  def initialize_agent():
      """Initialize the metacognitive agent"""
      global metacognitive_agent
      if metacognitive_agent is None:
          metacognitive_agent = MetacognitiveAgent()
      return metacognitive_agent


  @mcp.tool()
  async def metacognitivethinking(
      thought: str,
      thoughtNumber: int,
      totalThoughts: int,
      nextThoughtNeeded: bool,
      isRevision: Optional[bool] = None,
      revisesThought: Optional[int] = None,
      branchFromThought: Optional[int] = None,
      branchId: Optional[str] = None,
      needsMoreThoughts: Optional[bool] = None,
      metaObservation: Optional[str] = None,
      questionAssumption: Optional[str] = None,
      patternRecognized: Optional[str] = None,
      improvementIdentified: Optional[str] = None,
      redundancyDetected: Optional[str] = None,
      metacognitiveType: Optional[str] = None
  ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
      """
      Enhanced thinking tool with metacognitive awareness and integrated analysis.
      """
      global thought_history, branches, metacognitive_insights

      # Initialize agent if needed
      agent = initialize_agent()

      # Store thought
      thought_entry = {
          "thought": thought,
          "thoughtNumber": thoughtNumber,
          "totalThoughts": totalThoughts,
          "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
          "isRevision": isRevision,
          "revisesThought": revisesThought
      }

      # Handle branching
      if branchId and branchFromThought:
          if branchId not in branches:
              branches[branchId] = []
          branches[branchId].append(thought_entry)
      else:
          thought_history.append(thought_entry)

      # Store metacognitive insights if provided
      if any([metaObservation, questionAssumption, patternRecognized,
              improvementIdentified, redundancyDetected]):
          insight = {
              "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
              "type": metacognitiveType or "observation",
              "content": (metaObservation or questionAssumption or
                         patternRecognized or improvementIdentified or
                         redundancyDetected),
              "thoughtContext": thoughtNumber
          }
          metacognitive_insights.append(insight)

      # Call complete metacognitive agent (parallel analysis!)
      metacognitive_result = None
      metacognitive_error = None

      try:
          # Call metacognitive agent with full context
          metacognitive_result = await agent.analyze(
              thought=thought,
              thought_number=thoughtNumber,
              total_thoughts=totalThoughts,
              thought_history=thought_history,
              session_id="default"
          )

          # Log results
          if metacognitive_result:
              if metacognitive_result.get("violations"):
                  print(f"⚠️ Found {len(metacognitive_result['violations'])} violations", file=sys.stderr)
              if metacognitive_result.get("patterns"):
                  print(f"🔄 Detected {len(metacognitive_result['patterns'])} patterns", file=sys.stderr)
              print(f"⏱️ Analysis time: {metacognitive_result.get('analysis_time', 0)}s", file=sys.stderr)

      except Exception as e:
          metacognitive_error = str(e)
          print(f"❌ Metacognitive error: {metacognitive_error}", file=sys.stderr)

      # Build response with complete metacognitive analysis
      response = {
          "thoughtNumber": thoughtNumber,
          "totalThoughts": totalThoughts,
          "nextThoughtNeeded": nextThoughtNeeded,
          "branches": list(branches.keys()),
          "thoughtHistoryLength": len(thought_history),
          "recentInsights": metacognitive_insights[-3:],
          "insightCount": len(metacognitive_insights),
          "metacognitive": metacognitive_result,
          "metacognitiveError": metacognitive_error
      }

      return response


  @mcp.tool()
  async def getthinkinginsights() -> Dict[str, Any]:
      """Retrieve a summary of metacognitive insights collected during thinking sessions"""
      global metacognitive_insights, thought_history

      if not metacognitive_insights:
          return {
              "message": "No metacognitive insights collected yet. Insights are gathered during thinking sessions."
          }

      # Group insights by type
      insights_by_type = {}
      for insight in metacognitive_insights:
          insight_type = insight.get("type", "observation")
          if insight_type not in insights_by_type:
              insights_by_type[insight_type] = []
          insights_by_type[insight_type].append(insight)

      # Calculate session duration
      session_duration = None
      if thought_history:
          try:
              first = datetime.fromisoformat(thought_history[0]["timestamp"])
              last = datetime.fromisoformat(thought_history[-1]["timestamp"])
              duration = last - first
              session_duration = str(duration)
          except:
              pass

      # Build summary
      summary = {
          "totalInsights": len(metacognitive_insights),
          "insightTypes": list(insights_by_type.keys()),
          "recentInsights": metacognitive_insights[-10:],
          "insightsByType": {
              k: len(v) for k, v in insights_by_type.items()
          },
          "sessionDuration": session_duration
      }

      return summary


  # Run the server
  if __name__ == "__main__":
      mcp.run()
  '''
  )

  # Write back
  with open('.cursor/mcp.json', 'w') as f:
